-- specification EF (input = DH & output = OUT_TAlert)  is true
-- specification AG ((input = DH & output = OUT_TAlert) -> AX (output = OUT_TAlert | output = OUT_Alert))  is true
-- specification EF (input = DH & output = OUT_TAlert)  is true
-- specification AG ((input = NH & output = OUT_TWarning) -> AX (output = OUT_TWarning | output = OUT_Safe))  is true
-- specification EF (input = NH & output = OUT_TWarning)  is false
-- as demonstrated by the following execution sequence
Trace Description: CTL Counterexample 
Trace Type: Counterexample 
  -> State: 30.1 <-
    state = q0
    input = NL
    output = 1
    OUT_Warning = 5
    OUT_TWarning = 4
    OUT_TAlert = 3
    OUT_Safe = 2
    OUT_None = 1
    OUT_Alert = 0
-- specification AG ((input = NH & output = OUT_TAlert) -> AX ((output = OUT_TAlert | output = OUT_Warning) | output = OUT_TWarning))  is true
-- specification EF (input = NH & output = OUT_TAlert)  is false
-- as demonstrated by the following execution sequence
Trace Description: CTL Counterexample 
Trace Type: Counterexample 
  -> State: 31.1 <-
    state = q0
    input = NL
    output = 1
    OUT_Warning = 5
    OUT_TWarning = 4
    OUT_TAlert = 3
    OUT_Safe = 2
    OUT_None = 1
    OUT_Alert = 0
